### 2026-09-19 — PER-5 cached-notice stacking hardening and regression lock (deploy paused)

- Scope: the cached/offline notice ("Showing cached data while offline.") must stay readable above the file list in root and nested folders on desktop and mobile/PWA, routine background refresh must remain a small header/toolbar indicator with no banner and no row movement, and pull-to-refresh stays mutually exclusive with cache-only mode (`isPullToRefreshEligible` requires `!cacheOnlyMode`).
- Structural hardening: `.file-list-panel` now creates an isolated stacking context (`isolation: isolate`) so the sticky `.list-head` (`z-index: 2`) can never paint above sibling surfaces; `.state-banner-slot` has an explicit stacking relationship (`position: relative; z-index: 1`) above unpositioned `main` and below every overlay surface (app-bar 10, scrims 20–39, PTR 40, dialogs 120).
- Real defect fixed: the dead `.stale-info` span was `display: none` at every breakpoint, but its parent `.browse-context-row` still mounted whenever `staleInfo` existed, adding a phantom ~5.6px `.browse-header-main` grid gap that shifted file rows during routine refresh (measured in Playwright). The dead markup, the `buildStaleInfo` port path, and orphaned `.stale-info` CSS were removed; `.browse-context-row` now mounts only when `searchActive`. The selection-details "Cached" metadata path (`buildWorkspaceStaleInfo` → `SelectionDetailsStage`) is intentionally preserved.
- New browser regression coverage in `apps/web/tests/responsive-layout.spec.ts`: `PER-5 cached offline notice stays readable above the file list` asserts notice-vs-panel geometry, `elementFromPoint` hit-testing, root+nested folders, post-scroll state, and PTR-suppression on mobile; `PER-5 routine cached refresh uses header status and never moves file rows` asserts zero `.banner-state`, per-project header status, and ≤1px first-row stability across a gated refresh. A temporary injected-overlap sanity check confirmed the geometry assertions detect paint-over before being removed.
- New checked-in evidence: `docs/screenshots/davora-mobile-cached-notice.png`, captured by the new `captures the cached-data notice above the mobile file list` scenario in `apps/web/tests/offline-transfer-screenshots.ts` and registered in the screenshot ownership authority; it shows the offline notice fully above the list with all rows readable.
- Validation: web Vitest 310 files / 2,479 tests; focused touched-file scope 6 files / 40 tests; typecheck (quality + all workspaces), production build, and lint (331 baselined findings, no new debt) all pass. Playwright 64 passed / 10 intentional project-partition skips across responsive-layout, offline, css-ownership, navigation, browsing, app, and app-cross-feature specs on desktop-chrome and mobile-chrome; screenshot ownership passes 2/2. System browser was `/usr/bin/chromium-browser` via `PLAYWRIGHT_CHROME_EXECUTABLE` (no bundled Playwright browsers installed).
- Independent review-refine pass returned `Verdict: APPROVED` with no must-fix findings; the two nits (an owner-shape declaration still consumed by the app presentation layer and a pre-existing redundant mobile CSS rule) were intentionally left unchanged.
- PWA suite: 5/6 pass including the built-PWA offline cached-data test. `update prompt reload applies changed build content` times out intermittently in this environment at the SW-update-detection step (the 2026-09-19 snapshot added axe checks inside its 60s budget); the identical failure reproduces on a clean-HEAD baseline worktree, confirming a pre-existing environment flake unrelated to this change set. It remains a known gate to revisit before the paused deploy proceeds.
- Deployment and Linear closeout are deliberately paused pending explicit user approval: no deploy, commit, push, tracker mutation, or external-state action occurred in this pass.

## Required UI evidence workflow
When UI work changes the shipped experience, rerun all of these from the repo root:
- `rtk npm run typecheck`
- `rtk npm test`
- `rtk npm run build`
- `rtk npm run test:pwa`
- `rtk npm run test:e2e`

`rtk npm run test:pwa` is the dedicated preview-build PWA verification path for manifest/installability/service-worker/offline behavior.

`rtk npm run test:e2e` is the full browser evidence path: the root script runs the Playwright browser suite first and then regenerates checked-in screenshots for desktop and mobile evidence.

UX/product conclusions must be drawn from that browser-rendered evidence path, not from source inspection alone. If a reviewer or agent has only read code, they may comment on implementation risk, but they must not approve or reject visual/interface quality without screenshots, browser output, or a browser walkthrough.

## Required deploy/runtime workflow evidence
When a change touches deployment wiring, Worker runtime config, or PWA update signaling, rerun these exact commands in addition to the usual automated tests:

- `cd apps/worker && wrangler dev`
- `npm run deploy:worker -- --dry-run`
- `npm run deploy:web`

Expected evidence rules:
- `wrangler dev` must start from `apps/worker` with the checked-in safe mock defaults once Wrangler is installed/on `PATH`, and should answer `GET /api/health` successfully.
- Worker deploy must be exercised through the real Wrangler bundle path with `--dry-run`, and release deploy must fail fast if required runtime secrets such as `SESSION_SECRET` are not provisioned.
- Pages deploy must be exercised through the exact upload command even if Cloudflare blocks the final external upload because auth, account access, or a missing project; in that case, document the exact blocker precisely instead of marking the path “validated” without evidence.
- Vite development mode should not be used as proof of release-update prompting; that remains the job of `rtk npm run test:pwa`.

### 2026-09-20 — Post-release fixes: auth-before-validation, e2e hardening, deploy pinning (commit `8dcdc3f`)

- Worker auth ordering: unauthenticated `GET /api/files?path=/` returned 500 `mutation_failed` because catalog input parsing ran before authentication and `invalid_file_query` mapped to an opaque 500. `matchWorkerRoute` now returns an `InvalidInputRoute` carrying the deferred failure; `createWorkerRequestContext` authenticates first (including `authorityToken` form credentials and stream-token path binding, which is skipped only when input already failed); `handleWorkerApplication` rethrows `inputError` after the context is established. `invalid_file_query` and `invalid_move_copy_json` now map to 400 `invalid_request`.
- Worker regression coverage: route-matrix gains unauthenticated `?path=/` and `?path=../escape` → 401 assertions; ingress characterization splits blank-path (400 after form-token auth) from blank-token (401 missing credential) POST-download cases; router test renamed to "rejects wrong methods and defers malformed input behind authentication" and the PRV-03 owner citation follows.
- Playwright main config serves a built `vite preview` instead of `vite dev`: measured dev-mode navigation cost was ~22s of module-graph transform under `domcontentloaded`+`load` on a quiet box (>60s under load), the root cause of the `page.goto`/`page.reload` timeouts. Dev-server PWA coverage (A32 contract) preserved in new `playwright.dev.config.ts` via `test:playwright` second phase.
- PWA suite isolation: `installed PWA opens with cached shell` no longer POSTs `/__davora/stop-worker` (which permanently killed the shared worker and cascaded `ECONNREFUSED` into later specs); it now aborts requests via `persistentContext.route("**/*")`. Update-prompt spec carries explicit `setTimeout(240_000)` for its in-test rebuild; pwa config webServer timeout raised to 300s.
- Deploy script: `resolveWebDeployConfig` always passes `--branch` (default `main`, `CLOUDFLARE_PAGES_BRANCH` override preserved) so wrangler no longer derives a Preview branch from the checkout; commit hash/message/dirty provenance derived from git when env overrides are absent, with injectable probe for tests.
- Web vitest on Node 24+: Node's stub `localStorage`/`sessionStorage` globals (undefined without `--localstorage-file`) shadowed jsdom's because vitest's `getWindowKeys` skips keys already present on the Node global. `poolOptions.forks.execArgv: ["--no-experimental-webstorage"]` in `vite.config.ts` and `vitest.focused.config.ts` removes the stub globals so jsdom installs real per-environment storage (shared-file leakage from a `NODE_OPTIONS=--localstorage-file` workaround avoided).
- Gates: lint PASS (331 baselined), typecheck PASS (quality + all workspaces), architecture PASS (507 modules / 0 cycles), depcruise PASS (847 modules / 0 violations), quality 19/19, deploy 10/10, worker 275/275, shared 83/83, web vitest 2,658/2,658 (331 files), build PASS all workspaces.
- Playwright: main suite **214 passed / 122 skipped / 0 failed** (first fully green run on this host; previously-flaky `browsing.spec.ts:10` and `responsive-layout.spec.ts:63` pass in ~7s/14s); PWA suite 7/7 including update-prompt rebuild (3.2m) and cached-shell (14.1s); dev config 1 pass + 1 project-scoped skip.
- Deployed: Worker version `8f37d4a0-5fbe-4ddc-bb97-5035e836afdf`; Pages Production `0369a83b` on branch `main` @ `8dcdc3f` (branch pinning verified live in the deployment list; the earlier `integration/all-stories` Preview trap cannot recur).
- Production verification: unauthenticated `/api/files?path=/`, `/api/files`, `/api/files?path=../escape` all → 401 `unauthorized` (the original defect); invalid bearer → 401; `/api/health` reports real `nextcloud` backend with `configLoaded: true`; `POST /api/mock/reset` → 404; canonical `davora.pages.dev` serves the deployed bundle `index-BRvna0oP.js`, HTTP 200.

Coverage: `browserPreviewAdapters.test.ts` original-payload + filename-discriminator cases; `retentionPreviewCacheAdapter.test.ts` retainedOriginal forwarding + real-repository round-trip (member keeps original, non-member keeps decoded JPEG); `openedFileRepository.test.ts` membership/mismatched-path selection cases; `AppImagePreviewIntegration.test.tsx` stale kept-offline HEIC open → refresh → stored blob stays the fetched original with original name/mime/blobSize. - 2026-09-23 Linear PER-72 image Fit/Fill rendering: the desktop photo viewer's Fill and Fit controls produced identical output — the `<img>` is a grid item of `.preview-media-stage`, and its automatic minimum size (`min-height: auto`/`min-width: auto` = the image's intrinsic aspect-derived size) clamped the box to the image's own aspect ratio, so `object-fit: cover` and `contain` painted identically and the box overflowed the stage (reported as "stretched across the width in either mode"). `.media-preview-image` now carries `min-width: 0; min-height: 0`, matching the existing `.media-preview-video` convention — the box stays pinned to the stage so cover crops and contain letterboxes. - 2026-09-25 folder-not-loading fix (user bug report `davora-bug-report-20260924092758.zip`): a 2xx `/api/files` followed by `folder.load` failure (`errorKind: failure`) was reproducible when a DAV `displayname` legitimately diverged from the href basename — the Worker emitted `name` from `displayname` while the browser trust boundary requires `basename(path) === name`, so one divergent entry (leading-space filename, NFC/NFD divergence, custom displayname) failed the whole folder as invalid. `metadataFromItem` now derives `name` from the canonical href-decoded path (displayname only when basename is empty), `propfind` skips entries whose href cannot be represented (invalid percent bytes, encoded separators, control chars, traversal, root escape) while valid siblings survive, and `getcontentlength` must be finite before it can emit a `size` (400-digit values serialized as `null` and failed the client schema as `invalid_response`). Structural href errors (non-DAV path, username mismatch) still fail the request. Coverage: `tests/nextcloud.test.ts` +6 regression cases through real `listFolder` (5 fail pre-fix; the structural-rejection guard passes both ways). Gates: `check:fast` PASS — lint (313 baselined), quality/web/Worker/shared typechecks, architecture 514 modules/0 cycles, depcruise 859 modules/0 violations, deploy/CLI 18/18, quality 19/19, web 2,867/2,867, Worker 291/291, shared 84/84, builds PASS. - 2026-09-26 HEIC preview fallback resilience + preview diagnostics (production report `d93e7886` — "HEIC not rendering", Firefox 156 Android, standalone PWA): session showed `/api/file` + `/api/file/original` 2xx then the download/get-original fallback with zero recorded error events. Root cause could not be reproduced on desktop — headless Firefox 156 decoded the official libheif fixture end-to-end through the production worker bundle (398,939-byte JPEG, 1280x854), pointing at a Firefox-Android-specific worker-canvas gap (`OffscreenCanvas` 2D / `convertToBlob`) — and the report carried no diagnostics because preview acquisition was never instrumented. `decodeHeicPreview` is now a serialized ladder: worker `heicTo(bitmap)` -> `OffscreenCanvas` encode (unchanged happy path); on canvas failure the decoded `ImageBitmap` is transferred to the main thread and encoded on a DOM `<canvas>` via `toBlob`; on any other worker failure the decode retries once on the main thread via lazy `import("heic-to/next")`; a worker timeout or deterministic pixel-limit rejection skips the retry so a hanging file cannot freeze the UI. The ~3MB lazy `heic-to-*` chunk is excluded from Workbox precache alongside the worker chunk. Preview acquisition is now wrapped by `wrapDiagnosticsPreviewSession`: `preview-open` action lifecycle plus `error.reported` with `area: "preview"`, a classified `errorKind` (`heic-decode-failed`/`heic-timeout`/`heic-pixel-limit`/`heic-size-limit`/`heic-disabled`/`heic-fallback`), and a bounded `detail` (new optional `error.reported` field, schema v2). Coverage: `lib/heicPreview.test.ts` 12/12 (6 fail pre-change — bitmap handoff, worker-failure retry, timeout-no-retry, permanent-no-retry, combined error), `diagnostics/instrumentation.test.ts` 13/13, `model.test.ts` 19/19, `tests/nextcloud.test.ts` 10/10 incl. the R1 control-char `displayname` regression. Real-browser probe (vite build of the real `decodeHeicPreview`, headless Firefox 156): normal mode -> worker encode (`ok:true`); `OffscreenCanvas` disabled in worker -> `{ok:"bitmap"}` handoff -> identical JPEG; worker module failure -> lazy main-thread decode -> OK; `Worker` removed -> graceful failure (heic-to's WASM runtime needs Worker internally -> existing fallback UI). Gates: `check:fast` PASS — lint (313 baselined), typechecks, architecture 514 modules/0 cycles, depcruise 859 modules/0 violations, deploy/CLI 18/18, quality 19/19, web 2,881/2,881, Worker 292/292, shared 84/84, builds PASS, precache 25 entries/3.37MB. Two pre-existing load-sensitive mounted-App suites gained explicit 30s `vi.setConfig` timeouts (documented precedent — they exceed the 5s default under parallel load, no assertion weakened). Deployed Worker `f3344a29-973a-425c-ad3f-7eaad59d712a` (build label `ef8ee35001bd-dirty`) and Pages deployment `e87f1d70` (`davora.pages.dev`). Live verification: `/api/health` 200 with `x-davora-api-contract: 2`; unauthenticated `/api/files` and `POST /api/diagnostic-reports` -> 401; canonical Pages 200 serving `index-DhuDGg17.js` containing the `ok==="bitmap"`/`Main-thread HEIC`/`heic-to-` markers; `heicPreviewWorker-C4KIpvvm.js` and `heic-to-qhb7Nppn.js` both resolve 200; `sw.js` precache references neither HEIC chunk; inbox pull returned only the two known reports (R1 pre-fix folder rejection, R2 this fix's trigger).
- 2026-09-27 Linear PER-97 portrait file-browser rework: portrait viewports wider than the 900px mobile breakpoint (e.g. the reported 1270x1954 window) left a tall dead area under the file list because `.file-list-panel` kept the landscape `max-height: min(72vh, 46rem)` cap, and batch-selection actions rendered twice — once as a squeezed wrapping `.browse-selection-row` inside `.browse-header` and again in the right context rail. The header selection row is removed entirely; `SelectionDetailsStage` is the single owner of batch actions, and `projectBrowsingSurfaceBindings` no longer projects `selectionSummaryLabel`, `onDownloadSelection`/`onKeepOfflineSelection`/`onCopyMoveSelection`/`onDeleteSelection`/`onClearSelection`, or batch capability props (the `appBatchSelectionDownloadBridge` characterization now locks this boundary with a Header-batch-reintroduction mutant). A `(min-width: 901px) and (orientation: portrait)` block makes `.shell` a `min-height: 100dvh` flex column, stretches `.workspace-layout` to a single row, turns `.file-browser-panel` into a flex column, and removes the file-list portrait max-height; `.workspace-rail` scrolls internally (`max-height: 100%`, `45dvh` in the 901-1200px band). Browser-rendered proof: `tests/responsive-layout.spec.ts` "PER-97 portrait workspace fills the viewport and renders a single selection action surface" measures no horizontal overflow, computed `max-height: none`, and the file-list bottom inside the viewport at 1270x1954 and 1024x1366, plus exactly one `Download selected` control and no selection summary in the browse header. Checked-in captures: `docs/screenshots/davora-portrait-browse.png` + `davora-portrait-selection.png`.
